            <div class="podium-item">
                <div class="podium-avatar bg-secondary text-white">@p2.Username[0]</div>
                <div class="rank-box rank-2">
                    <span class="h2 m-0">2</span>
                    <span class="text-sm">@p2.Username</span>
                    <span class="badge badge-light mt-2">@p2.TotalXP XP</span>
                </div>
            </div>
        }

        @if(top3.Count > 0) {
            var p1 = top3[0];
            <div class="podium-item" style="width: 180px;">
                <i class="fas fa-crown crown-icon"></i>
                <div class="podium-avatar bg-warning text-white" style="border-color: var(--gold-accent);">@p1.Username[0]</div>
                <div class="rank-box rank-1">
                    <span class="h1 m-0">1</span>
                    <span class="h5">@p1.Username</span>
                    <span class="badge badge-light mt-2" style="color: var(--gold-deep)">@p1.TotalXP XP</span>
                </div>
            </div>
        }

        @if(top3.Count > 2) {
            var p3 = top3[2];
            <div class="podium-item">
                <div class="podium-avatar bg-dark text-white" style="background: #92400e !important;">@p3.Username[0]</div>

    <!-- Others List -->
    <div class="row">
        <div class="col-lg-10 mx-auto">
            <div class="list-container">
                <table class="table table-borderless mb-0">
                    <thead class="bg-light">
                        <tr class="text-muted small uppercase font-weight-bold">
                            <th class="pl-4">Hạng</th>
                            <th>Học viên</th>
                            <th class="text-right pr-4">Thành tích</th>
                        </tr>
                    </thead>
                    <tbody>
                        @{ int rank = 4; }
                        @foreach (var user in others) {
                            <tr class="user-row">
                                <td class="pl-4 align-middle">
                                    <span class="h5 text-muted">#@rank</span>
                                </td>
                                <td class="align-middle">
                                    <div class="d-flex align-items-center">
                                        <div class="avatar-sm rounded-circle bg-indigo text-white d-flex align-items-center justify-content-center mr-3" style="width: 40px; height: 40px; background: var(--indigo-primary);">@user.Username[0]</div>
                                        <div>
                                            <div class="font-weight-bold text-indigo">@(user.FullName ?? user.Username)</div>
                                            <div class="text-xs text-muted">@@@user.Username</div>
                                        </div>
                                    </div>
                                </td>
                                <td class="text-right pr-4 align-middle">
                                    <span class="xp-badge-mini">@user.TotalXP XP</span>
                                </td>
                            </tr>
                            rank++;
                        }
                    </tbody>
                </table>
            </div>
        </div>
    </div>
</div>

            </div>

    <div class="container-fluid">

            <div id="questionGrid" class="ag-theme-quartz w-100"></div>

@section Scripts {
    <script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/ag-grid-community/dist/ag-grid-community.min.js"></script>
    <script>
        let gridOptions;
        let searchTimer;

        const columnDefs = [
            { field: "questionId", headerName: "ID", width: 80 },
            { field: "content", headerName: "Nội dung câu hỏi", flex: 2, sortable: false },
            { field: "xpValue", headerName: "XP", width: 100 },
            { field: "departmentId", headerName: "Phòng ban", width: 120 },
            { 
                headerName: "Thao tác", 
                width: 140,
                cellRenderer: function(params) {
                    if (!params.data) return '<div class="skeleton w-75 h-75"></div>';
                    return `
                        <button class='btn btn-xs btn-outline-indigo mr-1' onclick='edit(${params.data.questionId})'><i class='fas fa-edit'></i></button>
                        <button class='btn btn-xs btn-outline-danger' onclick='del(${params.data.questionId})'><i class='fas fa-trash'></i></button>
                    `;
                }
            }
        ];

        const dataSource = {
            getRows: function (params) {
                const searchTerm = document.getElementById('searchTerm').value;
                const requestData = {
                    startRow: params.startRow,
                    endRow: params.endRow,
                    pageSize: 20,
                    searchTerm: searchTerm
                };

                console.log('[AG Grid] Đang gọi API với params:', requestData);

                fetch('/api/assessmentapi/questions', {
                    method: 'POST',
                    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
                    body: JSON.stringify(requestData)
                })
                .then(response => {
                    if (!response.ok) {
                        return response.json().then(err => { throw err; });
                    }
                    return response.json();
                })
                .then(data => {
                    console.log('[AG Grid] Dữ liệu nhận được:', data);
                    params.successCallback(data.rows, data.lastRow);
                    
                    if (gridApi) {
                        if (data.rows.length === 0 && params.startRow === 0) {
                            gridApi.showNoRowsOverlay();
                        } else {
                            gridApi.hideOverlay();
                        }
                    }
                })
                .catch(error => {
                    console.error('[AG Grid] Lỗi tải dữ liệu:', error);
                    let errMsg = error.message || 'Lỗi không xác định';
                    if (gridApi) gridApi.hideOverlay();
                    Swal.fire('Lỗi API', 'Không thể tải dữ liệu: ' + errMsg, 'error');
                    params.failCallback();
                });
            }
        };

        gridOptions = {
            columnDefs: columnDefs,
            rowModelType: 'infinite',
            cacheBlockSize: 20,
            paginationPageSize: 20,
            animateRows: true,
            overlayLoadingTemplate: '<span class="ag-overlay-loading-center"><i class="fas fa-spinner fa-spin mr-2"></i> Đang tải dữ liệu từ Server...</span>',
            overlayNoRowsTemplate: '<span class="ag-overlay-no-rows-center text-muted">Không tìm thấy câu hỏi nào phù hợp.</span>'
        };

        $(function() {
            const gridDiv = document.querySelector('#questionGrid');
            gridApi = agGrid.createGrid(gridDiv, gridOptions);
            
            gridApi.setGridOption('datasource', dataSource);
        });

        function onSearchChange() {
            clearTimeout(searchTimer);
            searchTimer = setTimeout(() => {
                gridApi.setGridOption('datasource', dataSource);
            }, 500);
        }

        function edit(id) { location.href = '/Assessment/QuestionBuilder/' + id; }
        function del(id) { 
            Swal.fire({ title: 'Xóa câu hỏi?', icon: 'warning', showCancelButton: true, confirmButtonText: 'Đồng ý' });
        }
    </script>
}

@section Scripts {
    <script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/jquery-steps/1.1.0/jquery.steps.min.js"></script>
    <script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/canvas-confetti@1.6.0/dist/confetti.browser.min.js"></script>
    <script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/sweetalert2@11"></script>

    <script>
        const examId = @ViewBag.ExamId;
        let questions = [];
        let userAnswers = {};

        $(document).ready(function () {
            loadQuiz();
        });

        async function loadQuiz() {
            try {
                const response = await fetch(`/api/assessment/questions?examId=${examId}`, {
                    method: 'POST',
                    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
                    body: JSON.stringify({ startRow: 0, pageSize: 50 })
                });
                const data = await response.json();
                questions = data.rows;
                renderQuiz();
            } catch (error) {
                Swal.fire('Lỗi!', 'Không thể kết nối đến máy chủ.', 'error');
            }
        }

        function renderQuiz() {
            const wizard = $("#quiz-wizard");
            questions.forEach((q, index) => {
                const options = JSON.parse(q.options || '[]');
                let optionsHtml = '';
                
                options.forEach((opt, optIdx) => {
                    const label = String.fromCharCode(65 + optIdx);
                    optionsHtml += `
                        <label class="answer-option" onclick="selectOption(this, ${q.questionId}, '${label}')">
                            <div class="opt-letter">${label}</div>
                            <input type="radio" name="q_${q.questionId}" value="${label}">
                            <span>${opt}</span>
                        </label>`;
                });

                wizard.append(`
                    <h3></h3>
                    <section class="question-card">
                        <div class="d-flex justify-content-between align-items-center mb-4">
                            <span class="text-muted font-weight-bold uppercase">Câu hỏi ${index + 1} / ${questions.length}</span>
                            <span class="xp-pill">+${q.xpValue} XP</span>
                        </div>
                        <div class="question-title">${q.content}</div>
                        <div class="options-container">${optionsHtml}</div>
                    </section>
                `);
            });

            $("#quiz-loading").addClass('d-none');
            wizard.removeClass('d-none');

            wizard.steps({
                headerTag: "h3",
                bodyTag: "section",
                transitionEffect: "fade",
                labels: { finish: "Kết thúc & Nộp bài", next: "Tiếp theo", previous: "Quay lại" },
                onFinished: submitQuiz
            });
        }

        function selectOption(el, qId, val) {
            $(el).siblings().removeClass('selected');
            $(el).addClass('selected');
            userAnswers[qId] = val;
        }

        async function submitQuiz() {
            Swal.fire({
                title: 'Đang gửi kết quả...',
                html: 'Hệ thống AI đang chấm điểm và phân tích năng lực của bạn.',
                allowOutsideClick: false,
                didOpen: () => { Swal.showLoading(); }
            });

            try {
                const response = await fetch('/api/assessment/submit', {
                    method: 'POST',
                    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
                    body: JSON.stringify({ examId: examId, answers: userAnswers })
                });

                const result = await response.json();
                
                Swal.fire({
                    title: `Điểm số: ${result.score.toFixed(1)}%`,
                    html: `Chúc mừng! Bạn nhận được <strong>${result.xpEarned} XP</strong>.<br><small class="text-muted">Huy hiệu và chứng chỉ (nếu có) đang được xử lý ngầm và sẽ xuất hiện trong Hub vinh danh của bạn.</small>`,
                    icon: result.score >= 50 ? 'success' : 'info',
                    confirmButtonText: 'Xem Bảng Xếp Hạng'
                }).then(() => {
                    if(result.score >= 80) triggerConfetti();
                    window.location.href = '/Assessment/Leaderboard';
                });

            } catch (error) {
                Swal.fire('Lỗi!', 'Có lỗi xảy ra khi nộp bài.', 'error');
            }
        }

        function triggerConfetti() {
            confetti({ particleCount: 150, spread: 80, origin: { y: 0.6 }, colors: ['#4338ca', '#fbbf24', '#ffffff'] });
        }
    </script>
}

                <div class="mt-4 p-3 bg-light border-radius-sm" data-step="3" data-intro="Sau khi thỏa mãn điều kiện bên trên, học viên sẽ nhận được phần thưởng bạn thiết lập ở đây.">

@section Scripts {
    <script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/jQuery-QueryBuilder@2.5.2/dist/js/query-builder.standalone.min.js"></script>
    <script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/sweetalert2@11"></script>
    <script>
        function startTour() {
            playTing();
            introJs().setOptions({
                nextLabel: 'Tiếp tục',
                prevLabel: 'Quay lại',
                doneLabel: 'Hoàn tất'
            }).start();
        }

        $('#builder').queryBuilder({
            filters: [
                { id: 'score', label: 'Điểm thi (%)', type: 'integer', operators: ['equal', 'not_equal', 'greater', 'less'] },
                { id: 'video_watched', label: 'Tỷ lệ xem Video (%)', type: 'integer' },
                { id: 'course_completed', label: 'Khóa học hoàn thành', type: 'integer' },
                { id: 'days_active', label: 'Số ngày hoạt động liên tiếp', type: 'integer' }
            ]
        });

        $('#btn-get').on('click', function() {
            var result = $('#builder').queryBuilder('getRules');
            if (!$.isEmptyObject(result)) {
                playTing();
                Swal.fire('Đã lưu!', 'Luật game hóa mới đã được áp dụng.', 'success');
            }
        });
    </script>
}

// =========================================================================
// FILE: SmartLMS.Web\Views\CodingChallenge\Solve.cshtml
// =========================================================================

@model SmartLMS.Models.CodingChallenge
@{
    ViewData["Title"] = "Thử Thách Lập Trình: " + Model.Title;
}

@section Styles {
    <link rel="stylesheet" href="~/css/premium-ui.css" />
    <style>
        .editor-header { background: var(--premium-bg); border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.1); }
        .terminal-output { background: #000; color: #00ff00; font-family: 'Courier New', Courier, monospace; height: 150px; overflow-y: auto; padding: 15px; border-radius: 0 0 12px 12px; }
        .test-case-card { border-left: 4px solid #6c757d; transition: all 0.3s; }
        .test-case-card.passed { border-left-color: #28a745; background: rgba(40, 167, 69, 0.1); }
        .test-case-card.failed { border-left-color: #dc3545; background: rgba(220, 53, 69, 0.1); }
    </style>
}

<div class="container-fluid py-4 animate-in">
    <div class="row">
        <!-- Cột trái: Đề bài -->
        <div class="col-lg-4">
            <div class="glass-panel p-4 mb-4" style="height: calc(100vh - 120px); overflow-y: auto;">
                <h3 class="text-info mb-3"><i class="fas fa-code mr-2"></i> @Model.Title</h3>
                <div class="badge badge-primary mb-3">@Model.Language.ToUpper()</div>
                <div class="badge badge-warning mb-3">@Model.Points XP</div>
                
                <hr class="border-secondary" />
                
                <div class="description text-white-50">
                    @Html.Raw(Model.Description)
                </div>

                <h5 class="mt-4 text-white">Test Cases:</h5>
                <div id="test-cases-summary">
                    @foreach (var tc in Model.TestCases.Where(t => !t.IsHidden))
                    {
                        <div class="test-case-card p-2 mb-2 bg-dark rounded text-small">
                            <small class="text-muted">Input: <code>@tc.Input</code></small><br/>
                            <small class="text-muted">Expected: <code>@tc.ExpectedOutput</code></small>
                        </div>
                    }
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>

        <!-- Cột phải: Editor -->
        <div class="col-lg-8">
            <div class="glass-panel neon-border overflow-hidden">
                <div class="editor-header p-3 d-flex justify-content-between align-items-center">
                    <span class="text-white-50 font-weight-bold">Solution.cs</span>
                    <button class="btn btn-success btn-sm px-4" onclick="submitCode()" id="btnSubmit">
                        <i class="fas fa-play mr-2"></i> Chạy & Nộp bài
                    </button>
                </div>
                
                <div id="monaco-container"></div>
                
                <div class="terminal-header p-2 bg-dark text-white-50 border-top border-secondary small">
                    <i class="fas fa-terminal mr-2"></i> OUTPUT TERMINAL
                </div>
                <div id="terminal" class="terminal-output">
                    > Sẵn sàng. Nhấn 'Chạy & Nộp bài' để kiểm tra kết quả...
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    </div>
</div>

@section Scripts {
    <script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/monaco-editor/0.36.1/min/vs/loader.min.js"></script>
    <script>
        require.config({ paths: { 'vs': 'https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/monaco-editor/0.36.1/min/vs' }});
        let editor;

        require(['vs/editor/editor.main'], function() {
            editor = monaco.editor.create(document.getElementById('monaco-container'), {
                value: `@Html.Raw(Model.TemplateCode ?? "// Viết code của bạn ở đây\n")`,
                language: '@Model.Language',
                theme: 'vs-dark',
                automaticLayout: true,
                fontSize: 14,
                minimap: { enabled: false }
            });
        });

        async function submitCode() {
            const btn = document.getElementById('btnSubmit');
            const term = document.getElementById('terminal');
            
            btn.disabled = true;
            btn.innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-spinner fa-spin mr-2"></i> Đang biên dịch...';
            term.innerHTML = '> Đang kết nối Sandbox...\n> Đang chạy Test Cases...';

            try {
                const response = await fetch('/CodingChallenge/Submit', {
                    method: 'POST',
                    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
                    body: JSON.stringify({
                        challengeId: @Model.Id,
                        code: editor.getValue()
                    })
                });

                const result = await response.json();
                renderResult(result);
            } catch (error) {
                term.innerHTML += '\n[LỖI] Không thể kết nối đến máy chủ.';
            } finally {
                btn.disabled = false;
                btn.innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-play mr-2"></i> Chạy & Nộp bài';
            }
        }

        function renderResult(result) {
            const term = document.getElementById('terminal');
            term.innerHTML = `> KẾT QUẢ: ${result.message}\n`;
            
            if (result.success && result.testCaseResults.every(t => t.passed)) {
                term.innerHTML += '> [CHÚC MỪNG] Bạn đã vượt qua tất cả bài kiểm tra!\n';
                triggerConfetti();
                Swal.fire('Thành công!', 'Bạn đã hoàn thành thử thách!', 'success');
            } else {
                term.innerHTML += '> [CẢNH BÁO] Một số bài kiểm tra bị thất bại.\n';
                result.testCaseResults.forEach((tc, idx) => {
                    if (!tc.passed) {
                        term.innerHTML += `> Test Case ${idx + 1}: Thất bại. (Thực tế: ${tc.actualOutput})\n`;
                    }
                });
            }
        }

        function triggerConfetti() {
            if (window.confetti) {
                confetti({ particleCount: 150, spread: 80, origin: { y: 0.6 } });
            }
        }
    </script>
    <script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/canvas-confetti@1.6.0/dist/confetti.browser.min.js"></script>
}

                                </thead>
                                <tbody>
                                    @foreach (var tc in Model.TestCases)
                                    {
                                        <tr>
                                            <td><code>@tc.Input</code></td>
                                            <td class="text-success font-weight-bold">@tc.ExpectedOutput</td>
                                            <td>
                                                <button class="btn btn-xs btn-danger" onclick="toastr.info('Đang xóa test case...'); $.post('/CodingChallengeManagement/DeleteTestCase', { id: @tc.Id }, function() { toastr.success('Đã xóa test case thành công!'); location.reload(); })"><i class="fas fa-trash"></i></button>
                                            </td>
                                        </tr>
                                    }
                                    @if (!Model.TestCases.Any())
                                    {
                                        <tr>
                                            <td colspan="3" class="text-center text-muted italic p-3">Chưa có test case nào.</td>
                                        </tr>
                                    }
                                </tbody>
                            </table>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    </div>
</section>

    .btn-indigo { background-color: #312e81; color: white; }
    .btn-indigo:hover { background-color: #4338ca; color: white; }

    .border-dashed { cursor: pointer; }
    .border-dashed:hover { background-color: #f8fafc; border-color: #10b981 !important; }

@section Scripts {
    <script>
        function openAddModal() {
            $('#cohortId').val('');
            $('#cohortName').val('');
            $('#cohortDesc').val('');
            $('#modalTitle').text('Tạo Lớp học (Cohort) Mới');
            $('#modalCohort').modal('show');
        }

        function editCohort(id, name, desc) {
            $('#cohortId').val(id);
            $('#cohortName').val(name);
            $('#cohortDesc').val(desc);
            $('#modalTitle').text('Chỉnh sửa Lớp học');
            $('#modalCohort').modal('show');
        }

        function saveCohort() {
            let id = $('#cohortId').val();
            let name = $('#cohortName').val();
            let desc = $('#cohortDesc').val();
            
            if(!name) { Swal.fire('Lỗi!', 'Vui lòng nhập tên lớp học.', 'error'); return; }

            let url = id ? '/Cohort/Update' : '/Cohort/Create';
            let data = id ? { id: id, name: name, description: desc } : { name: name, description: desc };

            $.post(url, data, function(res) {
                if(res.success) {
                    Swal.fire('Thành công!', 'Dữ liệu đã được lưu.', 'success').then(() => location.reload());
                }
            });
        }

        function deleteCohort(id) {
            Swal.fire({
                title: 'Xóa lớp học?',
                text: "Dữ liệu sinh viên sẽ được giữ lại nhưng lớp sẽ bị ẩn.",
                icon: 'warning',
                showCancelButton: true,
                confirmButtonColor: '#d33',
                confirmButtonText: 'Đồng ý xóa'
            }).then((result) => {
                if (result.isConfirmed) {
                    $.post('/Cohort/Delete', { id: id }, function(res) {
                        if(res.success) Swal.fire('Đã xóa!', 'Lớp học đã được chuyển vào thùng rác.', 'success').then(() => location.reload());
                    });
                }
            });
        }

        function openImportModal(id, name) {
            $('#importCohortId').val(id);
            $('#modalImport').modal('show');
        }

        $('#importForm').submit(function(e) {
            e.preventDefault();
            let formData = new FormData(this);
            Swal.fire({ title: 'Đang xử lý...', text: 'Vui lòng đợi trong giây lát', allowOutsideClick: false, didOpen: () => Swal.showLoading() });

            $.ajax({
                url: '/Cohort/ImportExcel',
                type: 'POST',
                data: formData,
                processData: false,
                contentType: false,
                success: function(res) {
                    Swal.fire('Xong!', res.message, 'success').then(() => location.reload());
                }
            });
        });
    </script>
}

                                        <button type="button" class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-primary rounded-pill" data-toggle="modal" data-target="#modalAddMember" onclick="$('#modalAddMember').modal('show'); toastr.info('Đang mở form thêm sinh viên...');">

<!-- Modal Add Member -->
<div class="modal fade" id="modalAddMember" tabindex="-1" role="dialog">
    <div class="modal-dialog" role="document">
        <div class="modal-content">
            <div class="modal-header">
                <h5 class="modal-title">Thêm sinh viên vào lớp</h5>
                <button type="button" class="close" data-dismiss="modal" onclick="$('#modalAddMember').modal('hide');"><span>&times;</span></button>
            </div>
            <div class="modal-body">
                <div class="form-group">
                    <label>Tìm kiếm Sinh viên</label>
                    <select id="userSelect" class="form-control select2-modal" style="width: 100%;">
                        <option value="">Nhập tên hoặc username...</option>
                    </select>
                </div>
            </div>
            <div class="modal-footer">
                <button type="button" class="btn btn-secondary" data-dismiss="modal" onclick="$('#modalAddMember').modal('hide');">Hủy</button>
                <button type="button" onclick="addMember()" class="btn btn-primary">Xác nhận thêm</button>
            </div>
        </div>
    </div>
</div>

@section Scripts {
    <script>
        $(document).ready(function() {
            // Initialize Select2 with AJAX search
            $('.select2-modal').select2({
                theme: 'bootstrap4',
                dropdownParent: $('#modalAddMember'),
                ajax: {
                    url: '/UserManagement/Search',
                    dataType: 'json',
                    delay: 250,
                    data: function (params) {
                        return { term: params.term };
                    },
                    processResults: function (data) {
                        return { results: data };
                    },
                    cache: true
                },
                placeholder: 'Nhập tên hoặc username sinh viên...',
                minimumInputLength: 2
            });

            // Init Lottie Empty State if div exists
            if (document.getElementById('lottie-empty')) {
                lottie.loadAnimation({
                    container: document.getElementById('lottie-empty'),
                    renderer: 'svg',
                    loop: true,
                    autoplay: true,
                    path: 'https://assets9.lottiefiles.com/packages/lf20_glp9al9n.json' // Empty Box Animation
                });
            }
        });

        function addMember() {
            let uid = $('#userSelect').val();
            let cid = @ViewBag.CohortId;

            if(!uid) {
                Swal.fire('Chú ý!', 'Vui lòng chọn sinh viên.', 'info');
                return;
            }

            $.post('/Cohort/AddStudent', { userId: uid, cohortId: cid }, function(res) {
                if(res.success) {
                    location.reload();
                } else {
                    Swal.fire('Lỗi!', 'Không thể thêm sinh viên này.', 'error');
                }
            });
        }

        function removeMember(uid) {
            let cid = @ViewBag.CohortId;
            Swal.fire({
                title: 'Xóa sinh viên?',
                text: "Bạn có chắc muốn xóa sinh viên này khỏi lớp?",
                icon: 'warning',
                showCancelButton: true,
                confirmButtonText: 'Đồng ý'
            }).then((result) => {
                if (result.isConfirmed) {
                    $.post('/Cohort/RemoveStudent', { userId: uid, cohortId: cid }, function(res) {
                        if(res.success) {
                            location.reload();
                        }
                    });
                }
            });
        }
    </script>
}

@model IEnumerable<SmartLMS.Models.Coupon>
@{
    ViewData["Title"] = "Quản lý Mã giảm giá";
}

<div class="content-header">
    <div class="container-fluid">
        <div class="row mb-2">
            <div class="col-sm-6">
                <h1 class="m-0"><i class="fas fa-ticket-alt text-purple mr-2"></i>Quản lý Coupons</h1>
            </div>
            <div class="col-sm-6 text-right">
                <a asp-action="Create" class="btn btn-primary shadow-sm"><i class="fas fa-plus mr-1"></i> Tạo Mã Mới</a>
            </div>
        </div>
    </div>
</div>

<section class="content">
    <div class="container-fluid">
        <div class="card shadow-sm border-0">
            <div class="card-body p-0">
                <table class="table table-hover mb-0">
                    <thead class="bg-light">
                        <tr>
                            <th>Mã Code</th>
                            <th>Mức Giảm</th>
                            <th>Loại</th>
                            <th>Ngày Hết Hạn</th>
                            <th>Trạng Thái</th>
                            <th class="text-center">Thao Tác</th>
                        </tr>
                    </thead>
                    <tbody>
                        @foreach (var item in Model) {
                            <tr>
                                <td class="font-weight-bold text-primary">@item.Code</td>
                                <td>@item.DiscountAmount.ToString("N0")@(item.DiscountType == "Percentage" ? "%" : " đ")</td>
                                <td><span class="badge badge-info">@item.DiscountType</span></td>
                                <td>@(item.ExpiryDate?.ToString("dd/MM/yyyy") ?? "Vô thời hạn")</td>
                                <td>
                                    @if(item.IsActive && (item.ExpiryDate == null || item.ExpiryDate > DateTime.Now)) {
                                        <span class="badge badge-success">Đang hoạt động</span>
                                    } else {
                                        <span class="badge badge-danger">Hết hạn/Tắt</span>
                                    }
                                </td>
                                <td class="text-center">
                                    <button class="btn btn-xs btn-outline-danger btn-del" data-id="@item.CouponId" onclick="toastr.info('Đang yêu cầu hệ thống xóa mã giảm giá...')">
                                        <i class="fas fa-trash-alt"></i>
                                    </button>
                                </td>
                            </tr>
                        }
                    </tbody>
                </table>
            </div>
        </div>
    </div>
</section>

@section Scripts {
<script>
    $('.btn-del').click(function() {
        var id = $(this).data('id');
        Swal.fire({ title: 'Xóa mã giảm giá?', icon: 'warning', showCancelButton: true }).then(res => {
            if(res.isConfirmed) {
                $.post('/Coupon/Delete/' + id, function() {
                    location.reload();
                });
            }
        });
    });
</script>
}

@section Scripts {
    <link href="https://cdn.quilljs.com/1.3.6/quill.snow.css" rel="stylesheet">
    <script src="https://cdn.quilljs.com/1.3.6/quill.js"></script>
    <script>
        $(document).ready(function() {
            var quill = new Quill('#quill-editor', { theme: 'snow' });

            // Wizard Logic
            $('.btn-next').on('click', function() {
                var next = $(this).data('next');
                $(`#wizard-tabs .nav-link[data-step="${next-1}"]`).addClass('done');
                switchStep(next);
            });

            $('.btn-prev').on('click', function() {
                var prev = $(this).data('prev');
                switchStep(prev);
            });

            function switchStep(step) {
                $('.wizard-step').removeClass('active');
                $(`#step-${step}`).addClass('active');
                $('#wizard-tabs .nav-link').removeClass('active');
                $(`#wizard-tabs .nav-link[data-step="${step}"]`).addClass('active');
                window.scrollTo(0, 0);
            }

            // Sync Quill
            $('#form-wizard').on('submit', function() {
                $('#hidden-description').val(quill.root.innerHTML);
                Swal.fire({ title: 'Đang xử lý...', text: 'Vui lòng chờ trong giây lát', allowOutsideClick: false, didOpen: () => { Swal.showLoading(); } });
            });

            // Free switch
            $('#chk-is-free').on('change', function() {
                if(this.checked) { $('#input-price').val(0).prop('disabled', true); }
                else { $('#input-price').prop('disabled', false); }
            });
        });

        function previewFiles(input) {
            if (input.files && input.files[0]) {
                var reader = new FileReader();
                reader.onload = function(e) { $('#thumb-img').attr('src', e.target.result); $('#thumb-preview-wrap').removeClass('d-none'); }
                reader.readAsDataURL(input.files[0]);
            }
        }
    </script>
}

@section Scripts {
    <script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/sortablejs@1.15.0/Sortable.min.js"></script>
    <script>
        $(document).ready(function() {
            var courseId = @Model.CourseId;

            // Load Data
            loadCurriculum();

            function loadCurriculum() {
                $.get('/CourseManagement/GetTreeData/' + courseId, function(data) {
                    var html = '';
                    data.forEach(mod => {
                        html += `
                        <div class="module-item p-3" data-id="${mod.id}">
                            <div class="d-flex justify-content-between align-items-start mb-3">
                                <div>
                                    <i class="fas fa-bars handle"></i>
                                    <span class="font-weight-bold text-dark h6 mb-0">${mod.text}</span>
                                    <span class="badge badge-secondary ml-2">${mod.children.length} bài học</span>
                                </div>
                                <div class="btn-group">
                                    <button class="btn btn-xs btn-outline-primary btn-add-lesson" data-mod-id="${mod.id}" onclick="toastr.info('Đang thêm bài học vào chương...')"><i class="fas fa-plus"></i> Bài học</button>
                                    <button class="btn btn-xs btn-outline-danger btn-del-node" data-id="${mod.id}" onclick="toastr.info('Đang chuẩn bị xóa chương học...')"><i class="fas fa-trash"></i></button>
                                </div>
                            </div>
                            <div class="sortable-lessons ml-4" data-parent="${mod.id}">
                                ${mod.children.map(les => `
                                    <div class="lesson-item" data-id="${les.id}">
                                        <i class="fas fa-grip-vertical handle"></i>
                                        <i class="fas fa-file-video text-info mr-2"></i>
                                        <span class="flex-grow-1">${les.text}</span>
                                        <button class="btn btn-link btn-xs text-muted btn-preview-les" data-id="${les.id}" onclick="toastr.info('Đang mở trình xem thử bài học...')"><i class="fas fa-eye"></i></button>
                                    </div>
                                `).join('')}
                            </div>
                        </div>`;
                    });
                    $('#module-container').html(html);
                    initSortable();
                });
            }

            function initSortable() {
                // Modules
                new Sortable(document.querySelector('.sortable-modules'), {
                    animation: 150, handle: '.handle', ghostClass: 'sortable-ghost',
                    onEnd: function() { console.log("Modules reordered"); }
                });

                // Lessons in each module
                document.querySelectorAll('.sortable-lessons').forEach(el => {
                    new Sortable(el, {
                        group: 'lessons', // Allow moving lessons between modules
                        animation: 150, handle: '.handle', ghostClass: 'sortable-ghost'
                    });
                });
            }

            // Save All Logic
            $('#btn-save-all').on('click', function() {
                var updates = [];
                $('.module-item').each(function(mIdx) {
                    updates.push({ id: $(this).data('id'), order: mIdx });
                    $(this).find('.lesson-item').each(function(lIdx) {
                        var modId = $(this).closest('.sortable-lessons').data('parent');
                        updates.push({ id: $(this).data('id'), order: lIdx, parentId: modId });
                    });
                });

                Swal.fire({ title: 'Đang lưu...', didOpen: () => { Swal.showLoading(); } });
                $.ajax({
                    url: '/CourseManagement/UpdateHierarchy',
                    type: 'POST', contentType: 'application/json',
                    data: JSON.stringify(updates),
                    success: function() {
                        Swal.fire('Thành công', 'Thứ tự đề cương đã được cập nhật!', 'success');
                    }
                });
            });

            // Add/Edit node logic... (Placeholder for brevity, can expand easily)
            $('#btn-add-module').on('click', function() {
                Swal.fire({ title: 'Tên chương mới', input: 'text', showCancelButton: true }).then(res => {
                    if(res.value) { 
                        // Logic call API to Create Module... For demo, we just toast
                        Swal.fire('Info', 'Vui lòng thực hiện tính năng lưu sau khi thêm Node mới (Mock logic)', 'info');
                    }
                });
            });
        });
    </script>
}

@section Scripts {
    <link href="https://cdn.quilljs.com/1.3.6/quill.snow.css" rel="stylesheet">
    <script src="https://cdn.quilljs.com/1.3.6/quill.js"></script>
    <script>
        $(document).ready(function() {
            var quill = new Quill('#quill-editor', { theme: 'snow' });
            $('.btn-next').on('click', function() { switchStep($(this).data('next')); });
            $('.btn-prev').on('click', function() { switchStep($(this).data('prev')); });
            function switchStep(step) {
                $('.wizard-step').removeClass('active'); $(`#step-${step}`).addClass('active');
                $('#wizard-tabs .nav-link').removeClass('active'); $(`#wizard-tabs .nav-link[data-step="${step}"]`).addClass('active');
            }
            $('#form-wizard').on('submit', function() { $('#hidden-description').val(quill.root.innerHTML); });
        });
        function previewFiles(input) {
            if (input.files && input.files[0]) {
                var reader = new FileReader();
                reader.onload = function(e) { $('#thumb-img').attr('src', e.target.result); $('#thumb-preview-wrap').removeClass('d-none'); }
                reader.readAsDataURL(input.files[0]);
            }
        }
    </script>
}

@section Scripts {
<script>
$(document).ready(function () {

    // ── 1. DATATABLES INIT ──────────────────────────────────────────
    var table = $('#tbl-courses').DataTable({
        ajax: {
            url: '@Url.Action("GetCoursesJson", "CourseManagement")',
            type: 'GET', dataSrc: 'data'
        },
        select: { style: 'multi', selector: 'td:first-child' },
        columns: [
            // Checkbox column
            {
                data: null, orderable: false, className: 'text-center',
                render: function (_, __, row) {
                    return `<div class="icheck-primary d-inline">
                                <input type="checkbox" id="check-${row.courseId}" class="row-checkbox">
                                <label for="check-${row.courseId}"></label>
                            </div>`;
                }
            },
            // Thumbnail
            {
                data: 'thumbnailUrl', orderable: false, className: 'text-center',
                render: function (url) {
                    var img = url ? url : '/img/no-image.png';
                    return `<img src="${img}" class="rounded shadow-sm" style="height:35px;width:55px;object-fit:cover;" onerror="this.src='https://placehold.jp/100x100.png?text=No%20Image'" />`;
                }
            },
            // Title & Preview Link
            {
                data: 'title',
                render: function (val, type, row) {
                    return `<a href="#" class="text-dark font-weight-bold btn-preview" data-id="${row.courseId}" onclick="toastr.info('Đang mở bảng xem thử nhanh...')">${val}</a>
                            <small class="text-muted d-block mt-1"><i class="far fa-calendar-alt mr-1"></i>${row.createdAt}</small>`;
                }
            },
            // Instructor
            {
                data: 'instructorName',
                render: function (name) { return `<span class="text-muted"><i class="fas fa-user-tie mr-1"></i>${name}</span>`; }
            },
            // Category Badge
            {
                data: 'category', className: 'text-center',
                render: function (cat) {
                    var colors = { 'Web': 'primary', 'AI': 'danger', 'Mobile': 'success' };
                    return `<span class="badge badge-pill badge-outline-${colors[cat] || 'secondary'}">${cat}</span>`;
                }
            },
            // Sparkline (Trend)
            {
                data: 'enrollmentTrend', className: 'text-center',
                render: function (data) {
                    return `<span class="inlinesparkline" data-values="${data.join(',')}"></span>`;
                }
            },
            // Price
            {
                data: 'priceFormatted', className: 'text-right font-weight-bold text-dark'
            },
            // Status Toggle
            {
                data: 'status', className: 'text-center',
                render: function (st, _, row) {
                    var checked = st === 'Published' ? 'checked' : '';
                    return `<div class="custom-control custom-switch">
                                <input type="checkbox" class="custom-control-input toggle-status" id="tg-${row.courseId}" data-id="${row.courseId}" ${checked}>
                                <label class="custom-control-label" for="tg-${row.courseId}"></label>
                            </div>`;
                }
            },
            // Actions
            {
                data: 'courseId', orderable: false, className: 'text-center',
                render: function (id) {
                    return `
                        <div class="btn-group">
                            <a href="/Payment/Checkout?courseId=${id}" class="btn btn-xs btn-default" title="Thanh toán VNPay"><i class="fas fa-credit-card text-warning"></i></a>
                            <a href="/CourseManagement/Edit/${id}" class="btn btn-xs btn-default" title="Sửa"><i class="fas fa-edit text-primary"></i></a>
                            <a href="/CourseManagement/Curriculum/${id}" class="btn btn-xs btn-default" title="Đề cương"><i class="fas fa-sitemap text-info"></i></a>
                            <button class="btn btn-xs btn-default btn-delete" data-id="${id}" title="Xóa" onclick="toastr.info('Đang yêu cầu hệ thống ẩn khóa học...')"><i class="fas fa-trash-alt text-danger"></i></button>
                        </div>`;
                }
            }
        ],
        drawCallback: function () {
            $('.inlinesparkline').each(function () {
                var vals = $(this).data('values').split(',').map(Number);
                $(this).sparkline(vals, { type: 'line', width: '60px', height: '20px', lineColor: '#4338ca', fillColor: '#eef2ff' });
            });
            $('[data-toggle="tooltip"]').tooltip();
        }
    });

    // Custom Filters
    $('#filter-instructor').on('change', function() { table.column(3).search(this.value).draw(); });
    $('#filter-category').on('change', function() { table.column(4).search(this.value).draw(); });
    $('#filter-status').on('change', function() { table.column(7).search(this.value).draw(); });
    $('#btn-reset-filter').on('click', function() {
        $('#filter-instructor, #filter-category, #filter-status').val('').trigger('change');
        table.search('').columns().search('').draw();
    });

    // ── 2. QUICK PREVIEW (AJAX) ───────────────────────────────────
    $('#tbl-courses').on('click', '.btn-preview', function (e) {
        e.preventDefault();
        var id = $(this).data('id');
        
        // Open AdminLTE Control Sidebar
        if (!$('body').hasClass('control-sidebar-open')) {
            $('[data-widget="control-sidebar"]').ControlSidebar('show');
        }

        $('#preview-container').html('<div class="p-5 text-center mt-5"><i class="fas fa-spinner fa-spin fa-2x text-primary"></i></div>');
        
        $.get('/CourseManagement/GetDetails/' + id, function (html) {
            $('#preview-container').html(html);
        });
    });

    // ── 3. SELECT LOGIC & BULK ACTIONS ────────────────────────────
    $('#check-all').on('click', function () {
        if (this.checked) table.rows().select();
        else table.rows().deselect();
    });

    table.on('select deselect', function () {
        var count = table.rows({ selected: true }).count();
        if (count > 0) {
            $('#bulk-actions').removeClass('d-none');
            $('#selected-count').text(count);
        } else {
            $('#bulk-actions').addClass('d-none');
            $('#check-all').prop('checked', false);
        }
    });

    // ── 4. HANDLERS ──────────────────────────────────────────────
    window.handleBulk = function (status) {
        var ids = table.rows({ selected: true }).data().toArray().map(r => r.courseId);
        Swal.fire({
            title: 'Chuyển đổi trạng thái?',
            text: `Bạn sẽ cập nhật ${ids.length} khóa học sang '${status}'`,
            icon: 'info', showCancelButton: true, confirmButtonText: 'Đồng ý'
        }).then(res => {
            if (!res.isConfirmed) return;
            $.ajax({
                url: '/CourseManagement/BulkToggleStatus?status=' + status,
                type: 'POST', contentType: 'application/json',
                data: JSON.stringify(ids),
                success: function (data) {
                    if (data.success) {
                        table.ajax.reload();
                        Swal.fire('Thành công!', data.message, 'success');
                    }
                }
            });
        });
    };

    window.handleBulkDelete = function () {
        var ids = table.rows({ selected: true }).data().toArray().map(r => r.courseId);
        Swal.fire({
            title: 'Xóa hàng loạt?',
            text: `Hành động này sẽ ẩn ${ids.length} khóa học khỏi danh sách.`,
            icon: 'warning', showCancelButton: true, confirmButtonColor: '#d33', confirmButtonText: 'Có, xóa ngay!'
        }).then(res => {
            if (!res.isConfirmed) return;
            $.ajax({
                url: '/CourseManagement/BulkDelete',
                type: 'POST', contentType: 'application/json',
                data: JSON.stringify(ids),
                success: function (data) {
                    if (data.success) {
                        table.ajax.reload();
                        Swal.fire('Đã xóa!', data.message, 'success');
                    }
                }
            });
        });
    };

    // Soft delete & manual Toggle single remains compatible...
    $('#tbl-courses').on('change', '.toggle-status', function () {
        var id = $(this).data('id');
        var st = $(this).prop('checked') ? 'Published' : 'Draft';
        $.post('/CourseManagement/ToggleStatus', { id: id, status: st }).done(() => table.ajax.reload(null, false));
    });

    $('#tbl-courses').on('click', '.btn-delete', function () {
        var id = $(this).data('id');
        Swal.fire({ title: 'Xóa khóa học?', text: 'Dữ liệu sẽ được ẩn đi.', icon: 'warning', showCancelButton: true }).then(res => {
            if (res.isConfirmed) $.post('/CourseManagement/SoftDelete', { id: id }).done(() => table.ajax.reload(null, false));
        });
    });

    $('#btn-export-csv').on('click', function () {
        var rows = table.rows().data().toArray();
        var csv = 'Title,Category,Instructor,Price\n';
        rows.forEach(r => csv += `"${r.title}","${r.category}","${r.instructorName}","${r.priceFormatted}"\n`);
        var blob = new Blob(['\uFEFF' + csv], { type: 'text/csv;charset=utf-8;' });
        var link = document.createElement("a"); link.href = URL.createObjectURL(blob); link.download = 'courses.csv'; link.click();
    });
});
</script>
}

@section Scripts {
    <script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/apexcharts"></script>
    <script>
        $(document).ready(function() {
            loadAnalyticsData();

            function loadAnalyticsData() {
                $.get('/Dashboard/GetAnalyticsData', function(data) {
                    // Risk Table
                    let riskHtml = '';
                    data.riskPredictions.forEach(p => {
                        let badgeClass = p.riskLevel === 'High' ? 'badge-danger' : (p.riskLevel === 'Medium' ? 'badge-warning' : 'badge-success');
                        riskHtml += `
                            <tr>
                                <td><strong>${p.studentName}</strong></td>
                                <td>${p.courseName}</td>
                                <td>
                                    <div class="progress progress-xs" style="width: 100px;">
                                        <div class="progress-bar bg-info" style="width: ${p.progress}%"></div>
                                    </div>
                                    <small>${p.progress}%</small>
                                </td>
                                <td>${p.probability}%</td>
                                <td><span class="badge ${badgeClass} px-3 py-2">${p.riskLevel}</span></td>
                            </tr>
                        `;
                    });
                    $('#risk-tbody').html(riskHtml);

                    // Mistake Chart
                    const mistakeChart = new ApexCharts(document.querySelector("#mistake-chart"), {
                        series: data.commonMistakes.map(m => m.count),
                        labels: data.commonMistakes.map(m => m.type),
                        chart: { type: 'pie', height: 350 },
                        colors: ['#4e73df', '#1cc88a', '#36b9cc', '#f6c23e', '#e74a3b'],
                        legend: { position: 'bottom' }
                    });
                    mistakeChart.render();
                });
            }
        });
    </script>
}

@section Scripts {
    <script>
        $(document).ready(function() {
            lucide.createIcons();

            // 1. SignalR Connection Setup
            const connection = new signalR.HubConnectionBuilder()
                .withUrl("/dashboardHub")
                .build();

            connection.on("ReceiveNotification", (user, message) => {
                const Toast = Swal.mixin({ toast: true, position: 'top-end', showConfirmButton: false, timer: 3000 });
                Toast.fire({ icon: 'info', title: message });
                $('#live-alert').removeClass('d-none');
            });

            connection.start().catch(err => console.error(err.toString()));

            // 2. Initialize DataTables
            var table = $('#activity-table').DataTable({
                "paging": true, "searching": true, "ordering": true, "info": false, "autoWidth": false,
                "responsive": true, "pageLength": 5,
                "language": { "search": "Tìm kiếm:", "paginate": { "next": "Tiếp", "previous": "Trước" } }
            });

            // 3. Global Chart Instances
            let engagementChart, roleChart, radarChart;

            loadDashboardData();

            $('#btn-refresh').click(function() {
                $(this).find('i').addClass('animate-spin');
                loadDashboardData(true);
            });

            // 4. Load Data & Hub Logic
            function loadDashboardData(withAlert = false) {
                // KPI Stats
                $.get('/Dashboard/GetStats', function(data) {
                    $('#total-revenue-saas').text(data.totalRevenueSaas.toLocaleString() + ' đ');
                    $('#avg-progress').text(data.avgCompletionRate + '%');
                    $('#dropout-risk').text(data.dropoutRiskRate + '%');
                });

                // Engagement Chart (Real Data)
                $.get('/Dashboard/GetEngagementChart', function(data) {
                    if(engagementChart) engagementChart.destroy();
                    engagementChart = new ApexCharts(document.querySelector("#engagement-chart"), {
                        series: data.series,
                        chart: { height: 350, type: 'area', toolbar: { show: false } },
                        colors: ['#312e81', '#10b981', '#f59e0b', '#3b82f6', '#ef4444'],
                        fill: { type: 'gradient', gradient: { opacityFrom: 0.45, opacityTo: 0.05 } },
                        xaxis: { categories: data.categories },
                        stroke: { curve: 'smooth', width: 2 }
                    });
                    engagementChart.render();
                });

                // Role Distribution (Real Data)
                $.get('/Dashboard/GetRoleDistribution', function(data) {
                    if(roleChart) roleChart.destroy();
                    roleChart = new ApexCharts(document.querySelector("#role-chart"), {
                        series: data.series,
                        labels: data.labels,
                        chart: { type: 'donut', height: 350 },
                        colors: ['#4338ca', '#f59e0b', '#ef4444']
                    });
                    roleChart.render();
                });

                // Activity Table (Real Data)
                $.get('/Dashboard/GetActivities', function(data) {
                    table.clear();
                    data.forEach(item => {
                        let riskClass = item.riskLevel === 'High' ? 'badge-danger' : (item.riskLevel === 'Medium' ? 'badge-warning' : 'badge-success');
                        table.row.add([
                            `<strong>${item.studentName}</strong>`,
                            item.courseName,
                            `<div class="progress progress-xxs mb-1"><div class="progress-bar bg-primary" style="width: ${item.progress}%;"></div></div><small>${item.progress}%</small>`,
                            `<span class="badge ${riskClass}">${item.riskLevel} Risk</span>`,
                            `<button class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-info rounded-pill px-3" onclick="showStudentDetail('${item.studentId}', '${item.studentName}', '${item.riskLevel}')">
                                <i data-lucide="eye" style="width:14px; height:14px;"></i> Chi tiết
                             </button>`
                        ]);
                    });
                    table.draw();
                    lucide.createIcons();
                    $('#btn-refresh').find('i').removeClass('animate-spin');
                });
            }

            // 5. Student detail (Explanable AI)
            window.showStudentDetail = function(id, name, risk) {
                $('#detail-fullname').text(name);
                $('#detail-email').text(name.toLowerCase().replace(' ', '') + '@@smartlms.edu.vn');
                $('#offcanvasName').text("Phân tích AI: " + name);
                
                // Radar Chart Data (Simulated AI Analysis based on Risk)
                let base = risk === 'High' ? [30, 20, 40, 50, 20] : [80, 70, 90, 85, 95];
                if(radarChart) radarChart.destroy();
                radarChart = new ApexCharts(document.querySelector("#radar-chart"), {
                    series: [{ name: 'Chỉ số', data: base }],
                    chart: { height: 300, type: 'radar', toolbar: {show: false} },
                    xaxis: { categories: ['Tiến độ', 'Tương tác', 'Điểm số', 'Chăm chỉ', 'Video'] },
                    colors: [risk === 'High' ? '#ef4444' : '#10b981']
                });
                radarChart.render();

                $('#risk-analysis-text').text(risk === 'High' ? 'Rủi ro cao do lượt đăng nhập thấp và tiến độ bài tập chậm.' : 'Hoạt động ổn định, có tiềm năng đạt chứng chỉ xuất sắc.');

                $('#studentOffcanvas').addClass('show');
                
                // Nudge logic
                $('#btn-nudge-real').off('click').on('click', function() {
                    let msg = $('#nudge-message').val();
                    if(!msg) { Swal.fire('Lỗi', 'Vui lòng nhập lời nhắn.', 'error'); return; }
                    
                    $(this).prop('disabled', true).html('<i class="fas fa-spinner fa-spin mr-2"></i> Đang gửi...');
                    
                    $.post('/Dashboard/SendNudge', { message: msg, email: $('#detail-email').text() }, function(res) {
                        Swal.fire('Thành công!', 'Email nhắc nhở đã được gửi qua Gmail.', 'success');
                        $('#studentOffcanvas').removeClass('show');
                        $('#btn-nudge-real').prop('disabled', false).html('<i data-lucide="send" class="mr-2"></i> Gửi Gmail Nhắc Nhở');
                        lucide.createIcons();
                    });
                });
            };

            $('.close').click(function() { $('#studentOffcanvas').removeClass('show'); });
        });
    </script>
    <style>
        .offcanvas { position: fixed; bottom: 0; z-index: 1050; display: flex; flex-direction: column; max-width: 100%; color: #212529; visibility: hidden; background-color: #fff; background-clip: padding-box; outline: 0; transition: transform .3s ease-in-out; }
        .offcanvas-right { top: 0; right: 0; border-left: 1px solid rgba(0,0,0,.2); transform: translateX(100%); }
        .offcanvas.show { transform: none; visibility: visible; }
        .bg-indigo { background: #312e81; }
        .bg-soft-primary { background: #e0e7ff; color: #4338ca; }
        .animate-pulse { animation: pulse 2s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0, 0.6, 1) infinite; }
        @@keyframes pulse { 0%, 100% { opacity: 1; } 50% { opacity: .5; } }
    </style>
}

@section Scripts {
    <script>
        $(document).ready(function() {
            // Polling Health API
            function checkHealth() {
                $.get('/health', function(data) {
                    if (data === "Healthy") {
                        $('#sql-status').html('<i class="fas fa-check-circle mr-1"></i> HEALTHY').removeClass('badge-danger').addClass('badge-success');
                    } else {
                        $('#sql-status').html('<i class="fas fa-exclamation-triangle mr-1"></i> DEGRADED').removeClass('badge-success').addClass('badge-danger');
                    }
                }).fail(function() {
                    $('#sql-status').html('<i class="fas fa-times-circle mr-1"></i> UNREACHABLE').removeClass('badge-success').addClass('badge-danger');
                });
            }

            setInterval(checkHealth, 30000); // Check every 30s
            checkHealth();
        });
    </script>
}

                        <input type="text" class="form-control bg-dark border-0 text-success fw-bold" id="rawKeyInput" value="@TempData["NewRawKey"]" readonly>
                        <button class="btn btn-outline-success" onclick="copyToClipboard()">
                            <i class="fas fa-copy"></i> Sao chép
                        </button>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    }

    <div class="card glass-card border-0 shadow-sm">
        <div class="card-body p-0">
            <div class="table-responsive">
                <table class="table table-hover align-middle mb-0 enterprise-table">

                        </tr>

                                    @if (key.IsActive == true)

                                    }
                                    else

                                        <span class="badge bg-danger-soft text-danger">Đã thu hồi</span>
                                    }
                                </td>

                                    {
                                        <form asp-action="RevokeApiKey" method="post" onsubmit="return confirm('Bạn có chắc chắn muốn thu hồi Key này không? Mọi ứng dụng đang dùng nó sẽ mất kết nối.');">

                                            </button>
                                        </form>
                                    }
                                </td>
                            </tr>
                        }
                    </tbody>
                </table>
            </div>
        </div>
    </div>
</div>

<script>
    function copyToClipboard() {
        var copyText = document.getElementById("rawKeyInput");
        copyText.select();
        copyText.setSelectionRange(0, 99999);
        navigator.clipboard.writeText(copyText.value);
        
        Swal.fire({
            title: 'Đã sao chép!',
            text: 'Hãy lưu giữ Key này cẩn thận.',
            icon: 'success',
            timer: 1500,
            showConfirmButton: false
        });
    }
</script>

@model IEnumerable<SmartLMS.Models.Permission>
@{
    ViewData["Title"] = "Ma trận Phân quyền (IAM Role Matrix)";
    var roles = ViewBag.Roles as List<string>;
    var rolePermissions = ViewBag.RolePermissions as List<SmartLMS.Models.RolePermission>;
}

@section Styles {
    <link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdn.datatables.net/fixedcolumns/4.2.2/css/fixedColumns.bootstrap4.min.css">
    <link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/overlayscrollbars/1.13.0/css/OverlayScrollbars.min.css">
    <style>
        .matrix-checkbox { transform: scale(1.5); cursor: pointer; }
        .permission-group { background-color: #f8fafc; font-weight: bold; }
        .DTFC_LeftBodyLiner { overflow-x: hidden !important; }
        .card-header { position: sticky; top: 0; z-index: 1000; background: white; }
    </style>
}

<div class="content-header">
    <div class="container-fluid">
        <h1 class="m-0 text-bold-xl"><i class="fas fa-shield-alt text-indigo mr-2"></i> @ViewData["Title"]</h1>
        <p class="text-muted">Quản lý quyền hạn tập trung theo từng nhóm vai trò (Role-based Access Control).</p>
    </div>
</div>

<section class="content">
    <div class="container-fluid">
        <div class="card shadow-sm border-0">
            <div class="card-body p-0" id="matrix-container">
                <table id="permissionMatrix" class="table table-bordered table-hover mb-0 w-100">
                    <thead class="thead-light">
                        <tr>
                            <th style="min-width: 250px;">Tính năng / Quyền hạn</th>
                            @foreach (var role in roles!)
                            {
                                <th class="text-center">@role</th>
                            }
                        </tr>
                    </thead>
                    <tbody>
                        @foreach (var group in Model.GroupBy(x => x.Group))
                        {
                            <tr class="permission-group">
                                <td colspan="@(roles.Count + 1)"><i class="fas fa-folder-open mr-2 text-primary"></i> @group.Key</td>
                            </tr>
                            @foreach (var p in group)
                            {
                                <tr>
                                    <td class="pl-4">
                                        <div class="text-bold">@p.Name</div>
                                        <div class="text-xs text-muted">@p.Description</div>
                                    </td>
                                    @foreach (var role in roles)
                                    {
                                        var isChecked = rolePermissions!.Any(rp => rp.RoleName == role && rp.PermissionId == p.PermissionId);
                                        <td class="text-center align-middle">
                                            <input type="checkbox" class="matrix-checkbox permission-toggle" 
                                                   data-role="@role" data-id="@p.PermissionId" 
                                                   @(isChecked ? "checked" : "")>
                                        </td>
                                    }
                                </tr>
                            }
                        }
                    </tbody>
                </table>
            </div>
        </div>
    </div>
</section>

@section Scripts {
    <script src="https://cdn.datatables.net/fixedcolumns/4.2.2/js/dataTables.fixedColumns.min.js"></script>
    <script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/overlayscrollbars/1.13.0/js/jquery.overlayScrollbars.min.js"></script>

    <script>
        $(document).ready(function () {
            // Khởi tạo OverlayScrollbars cho container
            $("#matrix-container").overlayScrollbars({ 
                className: "os-theme-dark",
                scrollbars: { autoHide: "leave" }
            });

            // Khởi tạo DataTable với FixedColumns
            var table = $('#permissionMatrix').DataTable({
                paging: false,
                searching: true,
                info: false,
                scrollX: true,
                fixedColumns: {
                    leftColumns: 1
                },
                language: {
                    search: "Tìm kiếm quyền:"
                }
            });

            // Xử lý Toggle quyền qua AJAX
            $('.permission-toggle').on('change', function() {
                var $chk = $(this);
                var role = $chk.data('role');
                var pId = $chk.data('id');
                var granted = $chk.is(':checked');

                $.post('/IAM/UpdatePermission', { role: role, permissionId: pId, granted: granted }, function(res) {
                    if(res.success) {
                        toastr.success('Đã cập nhật quyền cho ' + role);
                    } else {
                        toastr.error('Lỗi khi cập nhật quyền');
                        $chk.prop('checked', !granted);
                    }
                });
            });
        });
    </script>
}

@model SmartLMS.Models.Course
@{
    ViewData["Title"] = "Trình thiết kế Chứng chỉ (Interactive Designer)";
    var config = Model.CertificateConfigJson ?? "{\"fullName\":{\"x\":350,\"y\":250},\"date\":{\"x\":500,\"y\":450}}";
}

@section Styles {
    <style>
        #cert-canvas {
            width: 800px; height: 565px; /* Tỉ lệ A4 Landscape */
            background-image: url('@(Model.CertificateTemplateUrl ?? "https://img.freepik.com/free-vector/elegant-certificate-template-vector-with-professional-design-blue-gold-tone_53876-116528.jpg")');
            background-size: cover;
            position: relative;
            border: 2px solid #333;
            margin: 0 auto;
            box-shadow: 0 10px 25px rgba(0,0,0,0.2);
        }
        .drag-element {
            position: absolute;
            padding: 10px;
            background: rgba(67, 56, 202, 0.1);
            border: 2px dashed #4338ca;
            color: #4338ca;
            font-weight: bold;
            cursor: move;
            user-select: none;
            touch-action: none;
            font-family: serif;
            font-size: 24px;
        }
        .drag-element:after {
            content: " ⇳"; font-size: 0.8em; opacity: 0.5;
        }
        .sidebar-config { background: #f8fafc; border-left: 1px solid #e2e8f0; height: 565px; }
    </style>
}

<div class="content-header">
    <div class="container-fluid">
        <h1 class="m-0 text-bold-xl"><i class="fas fa-palette text-pink mr-2"></i> @ViewData["Title"]</h1>

    </div>
</div>

<section class="content">
    <div class="container-fluid">
        <div class="row">
            <div class="col-md-9">
                <div id="cert-canvas">
                    <div id="drag-fullname" class="drag-element" data-key="fullName">NGUYỄN VĂN A</div>
                    <div id="drag-date" class="drag-element" data-key="date">15/04/2026</div>
                    <div id="drag-course" class="drag-element" data-key="courseName">@Model.Title</div>
                </div>
            </div>
            <div class="col-md-3 sidebar-config p-4">
                <h5><i class="fas fa-cog mr-2"></i> Cấu hình</h5>
                <hr>
                <div class="form-group">
                    <label>Ảnh phôi (URL)</label>
                    <input type="text" class="form-control" id="templateUrl" value="@Model.CertificateTemplateUrl">
                </div>
                <div id="coords-log" class="small text-monospace bg-dark text-success p-2 rounded mb-3">
                    Đang chờ thay đổi...
                </div>
                <button class="btn btn-primary btn-block" id="btnSaveDesign" onclick="toastr.info('Đang lưu tọa độ cấu hình phôi bằng...');">
                    <i class="fas fa-save mr-2"></i> Lưu Tọa Độ
                </button>
            </div>
        </div>
    </div>
</section>

@section Scripts {
    <!-- Interact.js Core -->
    <script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/interactjs/dist/interact.min.js"></script>

    <script>
        var currentConfig = @Html.Raw(config);

        $(document).ready(function() {
            // Khởi tạo vị trí ban đầu từ DB
            applyConfig(currentConfig);

            // Cấu hình Interact.js
            interact('.drag-element').draggable({
                inertia: true,
                modifiers: [
                    interact.modifiers.restrictRect({
                        restriction: 'parent',
                        endOnly: true
                    })
                ],
                autoScroll: true,
                listeners: {
                    move: dragMoveListener,
                    end: function(event) {
                        updateLog();
                    }
                }
            });

            function dragMoveListener(event) {
                var target = event.target;
                var x = (parseFloat(target.getAttribute('data-x')) || 0) + event.dx;
                var y = (parseFloat(target.getAttribute('data-y')) || 0) + event.dy;

                target.style.transform = 'translate(' + x + 'px, ' + y + 'px)';
                target.setAttribute('data-x', x);
                target.setAttribute('data-y', y);

                // Cập nhật tọa độ vào Object
                var key = target.getAttribute('data-key');
                currentConfig[key] = { x: x, y: y };
            }

            function applyConfig(cfg) {
                Object.keys(cfg).forEach(key => {
                    var el = $('.drag-element[data-key="' + key + '"]')[0];
                    if (el) {
                        var x = cfg[key].x;
                        var y = cfg[key].y;
                        el.style.transform = 'translate(' + x + 'px, ' + y + 'px)';
                        el.setAttribute('data-x', x);
                        el.setAttribute('data-y', y);
                    }
                });
                updateLog();
            }

            function updateLog() {
                $('#coords-log').html(JSON.stringify(currentConfig, null, 2).replace(/\n/g, '<br>').replace(/ /g, '&nbsp;'));
            }

            $('#btnSaveDesign').click(function() {
                var courseId = @Model.CourseId;
                var json = JSON.stringify(currentConfig);

                $.post('/Marketing/SaveDesign', { courseId: courseId, configJson: json }, function(res) {
                    if (res.success) {
                        Swal.fire('Thành công!', 'Đã lưu cấu hình tọa độ chứng chỉ.', 'success');
                    }
                });
            });
        });
    </script>
}
